# Overview of system views

## Overview of views in the sys tenant

### Dictionary views overview

#### Security privileges

| View name | Description |
|-------|--------------------|
| [mysql.columns_priv](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/2990.mysql-columns_priv-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the column privileges granted to users. |
| [mysql.default_roles](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/3090.mysql-default_roles-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the default enabled roles of users. |
| [mysql.procs_priv](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/3510.mysql-procs_priv-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view stores the privileges of stored procedures and functions. |
| [mysql.role_edges](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/3590.mysql-role_edges-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the grant relationships between roles and users. |
| [oceanbase.CDB_OB_DATABASE_PRIVILEGE](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/4200.o-cbd_ob_database_privilege-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the database privileges of users in each tenant. |
| [oceanbase.CDB_OB_USERS](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/11900.o-cdb_ob_users-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the users and roles in all tenants of the cluster. |
| [oceanbase.DBA_OB_DATABASE_PRIVILEGE](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/18600.o-dba_ob_database_privilege-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the database privileges of users in the current tenant. |
| [oceanbase.DBA_OB_USERS](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/24100.o-dba_ob_users-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays the users and roles in the current tenant. |
| [oceanbase.CDB_OB_SENSITIVE_COLUMNS](300.system-view-of-sys-tenant/200.dictionary-view-of-sys-tenant/10430.o-cdb_ob_sensitive_columns-of-sys-tenant.md) | This view displays all columns protected by sensitive rules. |

#### InnoDB storage engine

| View name  |  Description |
|---------|----------|
| information_schema.NDB_TRANSID_MYSQL_CONNECTION_MAP       |  This view displays the mapping information between NDB transactions, NDB transaction coordinators, and MySQL servers attached to the NDB cluster as API nodes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_TRX       |  This view displays the information about active transactions. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_LOCK_WAITS  |  This view displays the information about locks that are waiting for conflicts. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_LOCKS       |  This view displays the information about all locks (waiting and held). <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_BUFFER_PAGE         |  This view displays the information about all cached pages in the buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_BUFFER_PAGE_LRU     |  This view displays the LRU information about pages in the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_BUFFER_POOL_STATS   |  This view displays the statistics about the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP                 |  This view displays the operation status information about compressed InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP_PER_INDEX       |  This view displays the operation information about compressed InnoDB tables and indexes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP_PER_INDEX_RESET |  This view displays the reset time information about operations related to compressed InnoDB tables and indexes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP_RESET           |  This view displays the operation status about compressed InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMPMEM              |  This view displays the status of compressed pages in the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMPMEM_RESET        |  This view displays the reset time status of compressed pages in the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_DATAFILES       |  This view displays the data file path information for each table and the general tablespace. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_INDEXES         |  This view displays the metadata information about InnoDB indexes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_TABLES          |  This view displays the metadata information about InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_COLUMNS |  The document does not provide a description of the function. |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_TABLESPACES     |  This view displays the metadata about InnoDB table files and the general tablespace. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_TABLESTATS      |  This view displays the low-level status information about InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_VIRTUAL         |  This view displays the metadata about virtual generated columns in InnoDB. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported and can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_TEMP_TABLE_INFO     | This view displays information about InnoDB temporary tables created by users. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_METRICS             | This view displays InnoDB performance information. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. </p></main>  |

#### Full-text index

| View name  |  Description|
|-----------|---------------------------------------------------------------|
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_BEING_DELETED  | This view displays the information about full-text indexes that are being deleted. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is provided only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported. You can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_CONFIG  | This view displays the configuration information of the InnoDB full-text search engine. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is provided only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported. You can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_DELETED  | This view displays the information about full-text indexes that are marked for deletion. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is provided only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported. You can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_INDEX_CACHE  | This view displays the cache status information of the InnoDB full-text indexes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is provided only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported. You can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_FIELDS       | This view displays the metadata information about the key columns (fields) of the InnoDB indexes, which is equivalent to the information in the SYS_FIELDS table of the InnoDB data dictionary. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is provided only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported. You can only query the table structure. </p></main>|

#### Foreign keys

| View name | Description |
|-----------|---------------------------------------------------------------|
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_FOREIGN      | This view displays metadata information about InnoDB foreign keys, which is equivalent to the information in the SYS_FOREIGN table of the InnoDB data dictionary. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>|
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_FOREIGN_COLS | This view displays status information about InnoDB foreign key columns, which is equivalent to the information in the SYS_FOREIGN_COLS table of the InnoDB data dictionary. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available only for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>|

### Dictionary views overview

#### Security and privileges

| View | Purpose |
|------------|--------------------------------|
| [mysql.db](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/3200.mysql-db-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays database-level privileges (VIRTUAL_TABLE). |
| [mysql.user](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/4200.mysql-user-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view records user-level privileges (VIRTUAL_TABLE).  |
| [information_schema.COLUMN_PRIVILEGES](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/700.information_schema-column_privileges-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays column privileges.|
| [information_schema.SCHEMA_PRIVILEGES](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/1900.information_schema-schema_privileges-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view describes database privileges.   |
| [information_schema.TABLE_PRIVILEGES](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/2700.information_schema-table_privileges-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view records table privileges.|
| [information_schema.USER_PRIVILEGES](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/2900.information_schema-user_privileges-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view records user privileges.    |
| [information_schema.CONNECTION_CONTROL_FAILED_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/200.information-schema-connection_control_failed_login_attempts-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the number of failed login attempts for different users.
| information_schema.ROLE_TABLE_GRANTS | This view displays the table-level privileges granted to activated roles for the current session, as well as the table-level privileges granted to other roles by these roles. |
| information_schema.ROLE_COLUMN_GRANTS | This view displays the column privileges granted to activated roles for the current session, as well as the column privileges granted to other roles by these roles. |
| information_schema.ROLE_ROUTINE_GRANTS | This view displays the routine privileges granted to activated roles for the current session, as well as the routine privileges granted to other roles by these roles. |
| oceanbase.DBA_OB_TRUSTED_ROOT_CERTIFICATE | This view displays the list of trusted CA root certificates in the OBServer cluster and their expiration times. |
| [oceanbase.DBA_OB_SENSITIVE_COLUMNS](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/11050.o-dba_ob_sensitive_columns-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays all columns protected by sensitive rules. |
| [oceanbase.DBA_OB_SENSITIVE_RULES](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/11070.o-dba_ob_sensitive_rules-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the definitions and attributes of all sensitive rules. |
| [oceanbase.DBA_OB_SENSITIVE_RULE_PLAINACCESS_USERS](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-mysql-mode/11060.o-dba_ob_sensitive_rule_plainaccess_users-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays all users or roles with plain-text data access permissions for columns associated with a sensitive rule. |

#### InnoDB storage engine

| View name  |  Description |
|---------|----------|
| information_schema.INNODB_LOCK_WAITS  |  This view displays information about the locks that are being waited on when lock conflicts occur. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_LOCKS       |  This view displays information about all current locks (waiting and held). <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.NDB_TRANSID_MYSQL_CONNECTION_MAP       | This view displays the mapping information between NDB transactions, NDB transaction coordinators, and MySQL servers connected as API nodes to the NDB cluster. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_TRX       |  This view displays information about active transactions. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_BUFFER_PAGE         | This view displays information about all cached pages in the buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_BUFFER_PAGE_LRU     | This view displays the LRU-sorted information about pages in the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_BUFFER_POOL_STATS   | This view displays statistics about the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP                 |  This view displays the operation status information related to compressing InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP_PER_INDEX       | This view displays the operation information related to compressing InnoDB tables and indexes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP_PER_INDEX_RESET |  This view displays the reset time information related to operations on compressed InnoDB tables and indexes. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMP_RESET           |  This view displays the operation status related to compressing InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMPMEM              | This view displays the status of compressed pages in the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_CMPMEM_RESET        | This view displays the reset time status of compressed pages in the InnoDB buffer pool. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_DATAFILES       | This view displays the data file path information for each table and general tablespace in the InnoDB file. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_INDEXES         | This view displays the InnoDB index metadata information. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_TABLES          | This view displays the InnoDB table metadata information. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_TABLESPACES     |  This view displays the metadata of InnoDB table files and general tablespaces. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_TABLESTATS      |  This view displays the low-level status information of InnoDB tables. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_VIRTUAL         |  This view displays the metadata of InnoDB virtual generated columns. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and you can only query the table structure. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_TEMP_TABLE_INFO     | This view displays information about active InnoDB temporary tables created by users. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_METRICS             | This view displays InnoDB performance information. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is available starting with MySQL 5.7. </p></main>  |

#### Full-text index

| View name  |  Description|
|-----------|---------------------------------------------------------------|
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_BEING_DELETED  | This view displays the information of the full-text indexes that are being deleted.<main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_CONFIG  | This view displays the configuration information of the InnoDB full-text search engine.<main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_DELETED  | This view displays the information of the full-text indexes that have been marked for deletion.<main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_FT_INDEX_CACHE  | This view displays the cache status information of the InnoDB full-text indexes.<main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>  |
| information_schema.INNODB_SYS_FIELDS       | This view displays the metadata information of the key columns (fields) of the InnoDB indexes, which is equivalent to the information in the SYS_FIELDS table of the InnoDB data dictionary. <main id="notice" type='explain'><h4>Note</h4><p>This view is for compatibility with MySQL 5.7. The related features are not supported, and only the table structure can be queried. </p></main>|

### Overview of performance views

#### ASH related

|  View name  |  Purpose|
|---------|----------|
| [GV$ACTIVE_SESSION_HISTORY](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/100.gv-active_session_history-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the active session history.|
| [V$ACTIVE_SESSION_HISTORY](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/30000.v-active_session_history-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the active session history.|
| [V$OB_ACTIVE_SESSION_HISTORY](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/30400.v-ob_active_session_history-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the active session history of the current OBServer node in the current tenant.  |
| [GV$OB_ACTIVE_SESSION_HISTORY](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/400.gv-ob_active_session_history-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the active session history of all OBServer nodes in the current tenant. |

| [V$OB_HMS_CLIENT_POOL_STAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/31150.v-ob_hms_client_pool_stat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the usage of the HMS Catalog client in the current tenant.|

| [V$OB_SSTABLES](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/34600.v-ob_sstable-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the SSTable information of a tenant.   |

#### Event-related

|View name|Description |
|--------|-----------------------------------------------------------|
| [GV$OB_PROCESSLIST](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/2500.gv-ob_processlist-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays session information on all OBServer nodes of the tenant.   |
| [GV$OB_TRANSACTION_PARTICIPANTS](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/4500.gv-ob_transaction_participants-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view displays transaction participant information on all OBServer nodes.    |
| [GV$SESSION_EVENT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/4700.gv-session_event-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view displays session-level waiting events on all OBServer nodes in the cluster.    |
| [GV$SESSION_WAIT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/4900.gv-session_wait-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays information about the current or previous waiting events of all sessions on all servers, including the event name and waiting time. |
| [GV$SESSION_WAIT_HISTORY](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/5000.gv-session_wait_history-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays information about the most recent 10 waiting events of all sessions on all servers.   |
| [GV$SYSTEM_EVENT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/5700.gv-system_event-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays waiting events at the tenant level in the cluster.   |
| [GV$SESSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/5100.gv-sesstat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays session-level statistical events on all OBServer nodes.   |
| [GV$SYSSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/5600.gv-sysstat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays tenant-level statistical events on all servers.  |
| [V$OB_PROCESSLIST](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/32900.v-ob_processlist-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view displays session information on the current OBServer node.|
| [V$STATNAME](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/36300.v-statname-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the definitions of all statistical events.   |
| [V$EVENT_NAME](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/30200.v-event_name-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view displays the definitions of all statistical events on the current OBServer node.   |
| [V$SESSION_WAIT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/35500.v-session_wait-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the details of the current waiting events of each session. Each waiting event has three parameters, and each parameter records a corresponding value. |
| [V$SESSION_WAIT_HISTORY](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/35600.v-session_wait_history-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the details of the current waiting events of each session and the most recent 10 waiting events.   |
| [V$SESSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/35700.v-sesstat-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the statistical events at the session level.  |
| [V$SYSSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/36400.v-sysstat-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays tenant-level statistical events on the current server.  |
| [V$SYSTEM_EVENT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/36500.v-system_event-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays tenant-level waiting event statistics.  |
| [GV$SESSION_LONGOPS](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/4800.gv-session_longops-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the execution status and progress of DDL operations in the cluster.   |
| [V$SESSION_LONGOPS](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/35400.v-session_longops-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the execution status and progress of DDL operations on the current OBServer node. |

|  View name   |    Description    |
|-----|------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| [GV$OB_PLAN_CACHE_STAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/2400.gv-ob_plan_cache_stat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the overall status of each plan cache on all OBServer nodes for the current tenant. |

| [V$OB_PLAN_CACHE_PLAN_STAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/32700.v-ob_plan_cache_plan_stat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view records the status of each cached object in the plan cache on the current server for the current tenant.  |

| [V$OB_PLAN_CACHE_PLAN_EXPLAIN](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/32600.v-ob_plan_cache_plan_explain-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the physical execution plans cached in the plan cache on the current server. |
| [GV$OB_PS_STAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/2700.gv-ob_ps_stat-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view monitors the overall status of the PS cache, including hit rate, memory usage, and the number of cached items.|
| [V$OB_PS_STAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/33100.v-ob_ps_stat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view monitors the overall status of the PS cache, including hit rate, memory usage, and the number of cached items.|
| [GV$OB_PS_ITEM_INFO](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/2600.gv-ob_ps_item_info-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view provides monitoring functionality for prepared statements, containing basic information about all prepared statements in the PS cache. |
| [V$OB_PS_ITEM_INFO](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/33000.v-ob_ps_item_info-of-mysql-mode.md)    | This view provides monitoring functionality for prepared statements, containing basic information about all prepared statements in the PS cache. |
| GV$OB_SESSION_PS_INFO | This view displays the PS information opened by all sessions for the current tenant. |
| V$OB_SESSION_PS_INFO | This view displays the PS information opened by all sessions for the current OBServer tenant. |
| [GV$OB_PX_TARGET_MONITOR](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/2900.gv-ob_px_target_monitor-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the usage of PX threads on all OBServer nodes. |
| [V$OB_PX_TARGET_MONITOR](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/33300.v-ob_px_target_monitor-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the usage of PX threads on the OBServer node. |
| [GV$OB_SQL_AUDIT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/3500.gv-ob_sql_audit-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the source and execution status of each SQL request on all servers.  |
| [V$OB_SQL_AUDIT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/34100.v-ob_sql_audit-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the source and execution status of each SQL request on the current server.  |
| [GV$SQL_PLAN_MONITOR](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/5200.gv-sql_plan_monitor-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the plan-level statistics of slow queries on all OBServer nodes. Each slow query has a corresponding statistic, and the trace information of the plan is recorded. |
| [V$SQL_PLAN_MONITOR](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/35900.v-sql_plan_monitor-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the operation-level statistics of slow queries. Each operation has a corresponding statistic.  |
| [V$SQL_MONITOR_STATNAME](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/35800.v-sql_monitor_statname-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the meaning of the numbers in the `OTHERSTAT_X_ID` column of `GV$SQL_PLAN_MONITOR`.  |
| [GV$OB_FLT_TRACE_CONFIG](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/900.gv-ob_flt_trace_config-of-mysql-mode.md) | This view displays the diagnostic information of full-link tracing. |
| GV$OB_PLAN_CACHE_REFERENCE_INFO | This view displays the plan cache reference information of all tenants on all OBServer nodes. |
| V$OB_PLAN_CACHE_REFERENCE_INFO | This view displays the plan cache reference information of all tenants on the current OBServer node. |

| [V$SQL_WORKAREA](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/36000.v-sql_workarea-of-mysql-mode.md)   | This view displays the workarea statistics for all operators that were executed under SQL automatic memory management. |

| [V$OB_SQL_WORKAREA_MEMORY_INFO](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/34400.v-ob_sql_workarea_memory_info-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view queries some overall information about the SQL workarea, including the maximum available memory, current used memory, and current held memory.  |

#### WR-related views

| View name   |  Description  |
|---------|------------|
| [GV$OB_QUERY_RESPONSE_TIME_HISTOGRAM](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/3050.gv-ob_query_response_time_histogram-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view queries the histogram information of the query execution time for all nodes in the current tenant.  |
| [GV$OB_RES_MGR_SYSSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/3050.gv-ob_res_mgr_sysstat-of-mysql-mode.md)  | This view displays the statistics of resource groups in all OBServer nodes of the current tenant. |
| [GV$OB_SQLSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/3900.gv-ob_sqlstat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the basic performance statistics of SQL statements in the cluster. Each SQL statement is represented by a row (each unique combination of `SQL_ID` and `Plan_Hash` is a row). |
| [V$OB_SQLSTAT](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/34500.v-ob_sqlstat-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the basic performance statistics of SQL statements on the current node. Each SQL statement is represented by a row (each unique combination of `SQL_ID` and `Plan_Hash` is a row). |
| [GV$OB_SESS_TIME_MODEL](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/3300.gv-ob_sess_time_model-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the session-level Time Model statistics in the cluster. |
| [V$OB_SESS_TIME_MODEL](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/33800.v-ob_sess_time_model-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the session-level Time Model statistics on the current node. |
| [GV$OB_SYS_TIME_MODEL](400.system-view-of-mysql-mode/300.performance-view-of-mysql-mode/4100.gv-ob_sys_time_model-of-mysql-mode.md)| This view displays the tenant-level Time Model statistics in the cluster.|

## Views in an Oracle tenant

### Dictionary views

  <main id="notice" >
    <h4>Applicability</h4>
    <p>This topic applies only to OceanBase Database Enterprise Edition. </p>
  </main>

#### Security privileges

| View | Description |
|----------------------------------------------------------------|-----------|
| [ALL_USERS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/6300.all_users-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays all database users visible to the current user.   |
| [DBA_USERS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/22500.dba_users-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays all database users.  |
| [USER_USERS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/32100.user_users-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays information about the current user.|
| [ALL_COL_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/500.all_col_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)   | This view displays column object privileges granted to the current user as the owner, grantor, or grantee. Column object privileges granted to enabled roles or PUBLIC are also displayed.    |
| [ALL_TAB_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/5300.all_tab_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)   | <ul><li>This view displays object privileges granted to the current user as the owner, grantor, or grantee.</li><li>This view displays object privileges granted to activated roles or the PUBLIC role.</li></ul>     |
| [DBA_COL_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/7400.dba_col_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)   | This view displays all column object grants in the database. |
| [DBA_SYS_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/20600.dba_sys_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)   | This view displays system privileges granted to users and roles. |
| [DBA_TAB_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/21400.dba_tab_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)   | This view displays all object grants in the database.  |
| [USER_TAB_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/31000.user_tab_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)   | This view displays object privileges owned by the current user.  |
| [USER_COL_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/25600.user_col_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays column object privileges granted to the current user as the owner, grantor, or grantee. |
| [USER_ROLE_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/29000.user_role_privs-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays roles granted to the current user. |
| [USER_SYS_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/30200.user_sys_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays system privileges granted to the current user.  |
| [ROLE_TAB_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/24500.role_tab_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays table privileges granted to roles. Only information about roles accessible to users is provided.|
| [ROLE_SYS_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/24400.role_sys_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays system privileges granted to roles. |
| [ROLE_ROLE_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/24300.role_role_privs-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays roles granted to other roles. Only information about roles accessible to users is provided.    |
| [DBA_ROLE_PRIVS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/18200.dba_role_privs-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays roles granted to all users and roles in the database.|
| [DBA_ROLES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/18300.dba_roles-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays all roles existing in the database.  |
| [ALL_DIRECTORIES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/1200.all_directories-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays all directories accessible to the current user.  |
| [DBA_DIRECTORIES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/8100.dba_directories-of-oracle-mode.md)  | This view displays all directories accessible to the current user.  |
| [DBA_OB_DATABASE_PRIVILEGE](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/12600.dba_ob_database_privilege-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays the privileges that the current tenant has over the current user. |
| [DBMS_LOCK_ALLOCATED](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/23700.dbms_lock_allocated-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays information about `DBMS_LOCK` that has been applied but has not expired. |
| [PROXY_USERS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/24250.proxy_users-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays proxy information between users. |
| [DBA_OB_USER_DEFINED_RULES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/16700.dba_ob_user_defined_rules-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view records the rules for rewriting SQL statements in the current tenant. |
| [DBA_OB_SENSITIVE_COLUMNS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/14130.dba_ob_sensitive_columns-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays all columns protected by sensitive rules. |
| [DBA_OB_SENSITIVE_RULES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/14132.dba_ob_sensitive_rules-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays the definitions and attributes of all sensitive rules. |
| [DBA_OB_SENSITIVE_RULE_PLAINACCESS_USERS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/14131.dba_ob_sensitive_rule_plainaccess_users-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays all users or roles with plaintext data access permissions for columns associated with a sensitive rule. |

#### Security policy-related

| View | Description |
|--------|----------|
| [ALL_POLICIES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/3000.all_policies-oracle.md) | This view displays the access control policies that the current user can access. When you apply access control to a table, you can create multiple policies for the table. |
| [ALL_POLICY_CONTEXTS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/3100.all_policy_contexts-oracle.md) | This view displays the context information of the views that the current user can access. When you access a view, the corresponding policy group is selected based on the context value. |
| [ALL_POLICY_GROUPS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/3200.all_policy_groups-oracle.md) | This view displays the policy groups of all views that the current user can access. The policy groups are combined with the policy context to apply different policies to different users. |
| [ALL_SEC_RELEVANT_COLS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/3800.all_sec_relevant_cols-oracle.md) | This view displays the columns associated with policies in the tables or views that the current user can access. When a column is associated with a column-level policy, the corresponding policy is applied when the column is accessed. |
| [USER_POLICIES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/28400.user_policies-oracle.md) | This view displays the access control policies that the current user owns. When you apply access control to a table, you can create multiple policies for the table. |
| [USER_POLICY_CONTEXTS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/28500.user_policy_contexts-oracle.md) | This view displays the context information of the views that the current user owns. When you access a view, the corresponding policy group is selected based on the context value. |
| [USER_POLICY_GROUPS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/28600.user_policy_groups-oracle.md) | This view displays the policy groups of the views that the current user owns. The policy groups are combined with the policy context to apply different policies to different users. |
| [USER_SEC_RELEVANT_COLS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/29300.user_sec_relevant_oracle.md) | This view displays the columns associated with policies in the tables or views that the current user owns. When a column is associated with a column-level policy, the corresponding policy is applied when the column is accessed. |
| [DBA_POLICIES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/17600.dba_policies-oracle.md) | This view displays the access control policies of all tables or views in the current tenant. When you apply access control to a table, you can create multiple policies for the table. |
| [DBA_POLICY_CONTEXTS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/17700.dba_policy_contexts-oracle.md) | This view displays the context information of all views in the current tenant. When you access a view, the corresponding policy group is selected based on the context value. |
| [DBA_POLICY_GROUPS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/17800.dba_policy_groups-oracle.md) | This view displays the policy groups of all views in the current tenant. The policy groups are combined with the policy context to apply different policies to different users. |
| [DBA_SEC_RELEVANT_COLS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/19400.dba_sec_relevant_cols-oracle.md) | This view displays the columns associated with policies in the tables or views of the current tenant. When a column is associated with a column-level policy, the corresponding policy is applied when the column is accessed. |

| [DBA_OB_BACKUP_DELETE_JOBS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/10800.dba_ob_backup_delete_jobs-of-oracle-mode.md)| This view displays the progress of the tenant-level backup deletion jobs.|

| [DBA_OB_BACKUP_DELETE_TASKS](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/11100.dba_ob_backup_delete_tasks-of-oracle-mode.md)| This view displays the information about the backup_set/backup_piece deletion tasks being executed in the current tenant.|
| [DBA_OB_BACKUP_DELETE_TASK_HISTORY](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/11000.dba_ob_backup_delete_task_history-of-oracle-mode.md)| This view displays the history of the completed backup_set/backup_piece deletion tasks in the current tenant.|
| [DBA_OB_ARCHIVELOG](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/10300.dba_ob_archivelog-of-oracle-mode.md)| This view displays the log backup status on each path.|
| [DBA_OB_ARCHIVELOG_SUMMARY](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/10500.dba_ob_archivelog_summary-of-oracle-mode.md)| This view displays the status of all log backups in the history and current.|
| [DBA_OB_ARCHIVELOG_PIECE_FILES](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/10400.dba_ob_archivelog_piece_files-of-oracle-mode.md)| This view displays the status of the pieces in the backup set.|

Each tenant can have only one load balancing job (JOB) running at a time. Each load balancing job generates multiple load balancing tasks (TASK) for specific operations. |
| [DBA_OB_BALANCE_JOB_HISTORY](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/12000.dba_ob_balance_job_history-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays the history of load balancing jobs executed in the current tenant. |

Each tenant can have multiple load balancing tasks (TASK) running at a time, and all tasks (TASK) belong to the same load balancing job (JOB). |
| [DBA_OB_BALANCE_TASK_HISTORY](500.system-view-of-oracle-mode/200.dictionary-view-of-oracle-mode/12200.dba_ob_balance_task_history-of-oracle-mode.md) | This view displays the history of load balancing tasks executed in the current tenant. |
